HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_132be95e83da824951bb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:5:2\",\"root_001028\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Verme kökündeki hizmet etme boyutu, alınan nimeti başkalarının ihtiyacına dikkatle karşılık verme kapasitesine dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_000569/B001","root_001028/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_147e2a3b65bb279a253f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:5:2\",\"root_001028\",[\"B002\"]],[\"93:5:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"93:5:4\",\"root_000569\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Vaat edilen veriş, tek seferlik bir karşılık değil, Rabbin aşama aşama sürdürdüğü bakımın hoşnutlukla sonuçlanan yeni bir evresidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000661/B001","root_001028/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2622315df1f8699a8c30","text":"3. **The root joins asker and giver in one circuit.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ط و:B005` is asking people for a gift, while its surface use activates `ع ط و:B002`, giving; 93:10 then supplies the `سَائِل` through `س ء ل:B001`. **Structural cue:** a promise to one who receives precedes a prohibition on repelling one who asks. **Assigned role:** the two `ع ط و` branches expose reversible social positions: today's recipient can face tomorrow's requester. **Abductive move:** I infer remembered receptivity → humane response to a request; the packet establishes the sequence but not the ethical causation explicitly. **Reading change:** satisfaction is not a closed endpoint, because the gift enters an ask/answer economy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002","root_001028/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3af4c9e997de47dd3118","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:5:2\",\"root_001028\",[\"B002\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aynı kökün hem verme hem hediye isteme yönlerini taşıması, bugünün alıcısıyla yarının isteyeni arasında rolleri değişebilen bir alışveriş bağı kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B005","root_001028/B004","root_001266/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5997e02b12105b17e66b","text":"4. **Overreach neutralized.** Evidence: ع ط و B004/B007 (taking on beyond right; outdoing in contest), ر ض و B005 (overcoming in the matter), ق ل ي B002/B003 in 93:3 (casting away; aversion), ق ه ر B001 in 93:9 (subduing). Structural cue: the same ayah could hold competitive/overreaching branches, but the grammar makes the Lord the giver and the addressee the satisfied recipient. Mechanism: the packet supplies contest branches, then later forbids oppressive response; I supply the reversal that legitimate gift cancels the need to seize or win. Abductive move: satisfaction comes when provision arrives without the recipient grasping beyond right. Reading change: the ayah reads as an antidote to anxious taking.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001028/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_725e1f5aeacd6eb91467","text":"93:6–8 confirmed the stage-by-stage role of `ر ب ب:B002`, but 93:9–10 produced the larger surprise: the gift's endpoint moved from satisfaction to service. The later `سَائِل` specifically activated fixed `ع ط و:B005`, exposing a reversible ask/give relation that prevents `فَتَرْضَىٰ` from closing the circuit around the recipient.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7a619579ba9a558b8191","text":"1. **The promised giving is one stage in sustained nurture.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ط و:B002` is handing over and giving, fixed `ر ب ب:B002` is nurture and completion stage by stage, and fixed `ر ض و:B001` is acceptance opposed to displeasure. **Structural cue:** 93:6–8 retrospectively enumerate shelter, guidance, and sufficiency in the same Rabb-centered sequence. **Assigned role:** giving is a concrete transfer; nurture links transfers across time; satisfaction names their recipient-side resolution. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies one future promise and three prior corrective acts; I infer that they are episodes of one continuing administration. **Reading change:** the ayah promises neither an isolated object nor a sudden payoff, but a further stage of a care process already evidenced.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B005","root_001028/B004","root_001028/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a57c771c4be8353e438e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:5:2\",\"root_001028\",[\"B004\",\"B007\"]],[\"93:5:4\",\"root_000569\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Hak sınırını aşma ve yarışta üstün gelme çağrışımları, meşru hediyenin kaygılı biçimde ele geçirme ihtiyacını ortadan kaldırdığı düşüncesini doğurur; bu karşıtlık doğrudan belirtilmez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a7f67dcc73e63b67481f","text":"93:9-10 changed the promise from a private future to an ethical template: عطاء received gently must become non-coercive conduct toward the orphan and asker. 93:11 then prevented \"satisfaction\" from closing inward; the satisfied recipient must still speak, so رضى becomes a settled base for narration rather than mute completion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a91951eecc85f4ece09b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:5:2\",\"root_001028\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Verişin yumuşakça eğilme boyutu, sonraki güç kullanma ve azarlama yasaklarıyla, ihtiyaca zorlamadan yaklaşan bir verme biçimini çağrıştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c0d8c648fb1f2c2b3837","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:5:4\",\"root_000569\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Karşılıklı razı olma boyutu, hoşnutluğu tek taraflı bir teselli olmaktan çıkarıp verenle alan arasında uyumlu bir sonuca dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d0aced84343144fd2c89","text":"3. **The giver also yields gently.** Evidence: ع ط و B006 (yielding and compliant ease), ه د ي B008 in 93:7 (supported/swaying walking), ق ه ر B001 in 93:9 (overpowering from above), ن ه ر B004 in 93:10 (harsh rebuke). Structural cue: promised giving is followed by prohibitions against domination and rebuke. Mechanism: the packet supplies a branch where عطو is pliancy, while later imperatives ban coercive handling of vulnerable figures; I supply the ethical transfer from how the addressee is given to how he must handle others. Abductive move: receiving from a non-coercive Lord becomes a template for non-coercive conduct. Reading change: \"He will give you\" includes a manner of giving: not overpowering, but bending toward need.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B003","root_001266/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_df194f86638c9d825845","text":"2. **Giving equips service.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ط و:B003` is serving by attending to a person's needs; 93:9–10 prohibit overpowering the orphan (`ق ه ر:B001`) and harshly rebuking the asker (`ن ه ر:B004`). **Structural cue:** the recipient of promised giving is immediately placed, later in the window, before two vulnerable social claims. **Assigned role:** the service branch converts received capacity into attendance to others. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies gift followed by commands; I infer gift → capacity for service, while the direction of that arrow is mine. **Reading change:** `يُعْطِيكَ` can prepare the addressee to become an attentive giver rather than terminate in private possession.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003","root_001525/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f2bed84ffe7117de1f17","text":"2. **Reciprocal consent rather than one-way appeasement.** Evidence: ر ض و B003 (mutual consent), ع ط و B002 (giving/handing), س ء ل B001/B003 in 93:10 (asking; fulfilling a request), ن ع م B004 in 93:11 (yes/affirmation). Structural cue: the later \"questioner\" and \"favor\" make giving part of a social exchange field. Mechanism: the packet supplies giving, asking, fulfilling, and affirmative response; I supply the reciprocity arrow that true satisfaction aligns giver and receiver. Abductive move: divine giving trains the addressee to receive and then answer others without rupture. Reading change: \"you will be satisfied\" becomes a relational settlement, not a private mood.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ff2a5af41a6a62259eea","text":"1. **Gift as completed nurture.** Evidence: ع ط و B002 (handing over/giving), ر ب ب B002 (repair, nurture, completion stage by stage), ر ض و B001/B004 (acceptance; making another satisfied). Structural cue: the line joins future giving, Lordship, and satisfaction in one chain. Mechanism: the packet supplies give → Lord → satisfaction; I supply the causal arrow that the gift is the next stage of Lordly completion. Abductive move: satisfaction is not a raw emotion but the sign that nurture has reached a fitting phase. Reading change: the ayah reads as promised completion of care, not merely promised compensation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The gift became a staged, need-responsive process whose completion is measured at the recipient rather than by transfer alone.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_9e4c169969f924d0d719","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
